\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_FRAG\_NEEDED@{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_FRAG\_NEEDED}!bpf.h@{bpf.h}}\index{bpf.h@{bpf.h}!BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_FRAG\_NEEDED@{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_FRAG\_NEEDED}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a55d44e5a031b72ee025954dbfb3091e4a1a30f28989665dabfa2edc528e35536f}\label{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a55d44e5a031b72ee025954dbfb3091e4a1a30f28989665dabfa2edc528e35536f}} 
B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+T\+U\+\_\+\+C\+H\+K\+\_\+\+R\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+R\+A\+G\+\_\+\+N\+E\+E\+D\+ED&\\
\hline

\raisebox{\heightof{T}}[0pt][0pt]{\index{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_SEGS\_TOOBIG@{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_SEGS\_TOOBIG}!bpf.h@{bpf.h}}\index{bpf.h@{bpf.h}!BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_SEGS\_TOOBIG@{BPF\_MTU\_CHK\_RET\_SEGS\_TOOBIG}}}\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a55d44e5a031b72ee025954dbfb3091e4a32d9ccf45e7bafffe6425743a44d55cc}\label{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a55d44e5a031b72ee025954dbfb3091e4a32d9ccf45e7bafffe6425743a44d55cc}} 
B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+T\+U\+\_\+\+C\+H\+K\+\_\+\+R\+E\+T\+\_\+\+S\+E\+G\+S\+\_\+\+T\+O\+O\+B\+IG&\\

\mbox{\Hypertarget{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a6c1895ffd031b5cb7d8f876de0ef5f2d}\label{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a6c1895ffd031b5cb7d8f876de0ef5f2d}} 
\index{bpf.h@{bpf.h}!bpf\_cmd@{bpf\_cmd}}
\index{bpf\_cmd@{bpf\_cmd}!bpf.h@{bpf.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{bpf\_cmd}{bpf\_cmd}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_a6c1895ffd031b5cb7d8f876de0ef5f2d}{bpf\+\_\+cmd}}}

D\+OC\+: e\+B\+PF Syscall Preamble

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} operation to be performed by the {\bfseries{bpf}}\textbackslash{} () system call is determined by the {\itshape cmd} argument. Each operation takes an accompanying argument, provided via {\itshape attr}, which is a pointer to a union of type {\itshape \mbox{\hyperlink{unionbpf__attr}{bpf\+\_\+attr}}} (see below). \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} size argument is the size of the union pointed to by {\itshape attr}. D\+OC\+: e\+B\+PF Syscall Commands

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE Description Create a map and return a file descriptor that refers to the map. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} close-\/on-\/exec file descriptor flag (see {\bfseries{fcntl}}\textbackslash{} (2)) is automatically enabled for the new file descriptor.

Applying {\bfseries{close}}\textbackslash{} (2) to the file descriptor returned by {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE}} will delete the map (but see N\+O\+T\+ES).

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+E\+L\+EM Description Look up an element with a given {\itshape key} in the map referred to by the file descriptor {\itshape map\+\_\+fd}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape flags} argument may be specified as one of the following\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Look up the value of a spin-\/locked map without returning the lock. This must be specified if the elements contain a spinlock.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+U\+P\+D\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+E\+L\+EM Description Create or update an element (key/value pair) in a specified map.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape flags} argument should be specified as one of the following\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+A\+NY}} Create a new element or update an existing element. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+N\+O\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} Create a new element only if it did not exist. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} Update an existing element. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Update a spin\+\_\+lock-\/ed map element.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

May set {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+I\+N\+V\+AL}}, {\bfseries{E\+P\+E\+RM}}, {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+M\+EM}}, {\bfseries{E2\+B\+IG}}, {\bfseries{E\+E\+X\+I\+ST}}, or {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.

{\bfseries{E2\+B\+IG}} \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} number of elements in the map reached the {\itshape max\+\_\+entries} limit specified at map creation time. {\bfseries{E\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} If {\itshape flags} specifies {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+N\+O\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} and the element with {\itshape key} already exists in the map. {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}} If {\itshape flags} specifies {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} and the element with {\itshape key} does not exist in the map.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+E\+L\+E\+T\+E\+\_\+\+E\+L\+EM Description Look up and delete an element by key in a specified map.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+K\+EY Description Look up an element by key in a specified map and return the key of the next element. Can be used to iterate over all elements in the map.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} following cases can be used to iterate over all elements of the map\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item If {\itshape key} is not found, the operation returns zero and sets the {\itshape next\+\_\+key} pointer to the key of the first element.
\item If {\itshape key} is found, the operation returns zero and sets the {\itshape next\+\_\+key} pointer to the key of the next element.
\item If {\itshape key} is the last element, returns -\/1 and {\itshape errno} is set to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.
\end{DoxyItemize}

May set {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+M\+EM}}, {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, {\bfseries{E\+P\+E\+RM}}, or {\bfseries{E\+I\+N\+V\+AL}} on error.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD Description Verify and load an e\+B\+PF program, returning a new file descriptor associated with the program.

Applying {\bfseries{close}}\textbackslash{} (2) to the file descriptor returned by {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD}} will unload the e\+B\+PF program (but see N\+O\+T\+ES).

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} close-\/on-\/exec file descriptor flag (see {\bfseries{fcntl}}\textbackslash{} (2)) is automatically enabled for the new file descriptor.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+P\+IN Description Pin an e\+B\+PF program or map referred by the specified {\itshape bpf\+\_\+fd} to the provided {\itshape pathname} on the filesystem.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape pathname} argument must not contain a dot (\char`\"{}.\char`\"{}).

On success, {\itshape pathname} retains a reference to the e\+B\+PF object, preventing deallocation of the object when the original {\itshape bpf\+\_\+fd} is closed. This allow the e\+B\+PF object to live beyond {\bfseries{close}}\textbackslash{} (\textbackslash{} {\itshape bpf\+\_\+fd}\textbackslash{} ), and hence the lifetime of the parent process.

Applying {\bfseries{unlink}}\textbackslash{} (2) or similar calls to the {\itshape pathname} unpins the object from the filesystem, removing the reference. If no other file descriptors or filesystem nodes refer to the same object, it will be deallocated (see N\+O\+T\+ES).

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} filesystem type for the parent directory of {\itshape pathname} must be {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+S\+\_\+\+M\+A\+G\+IC}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+G\+ET Description Open a file descriptor for the e\+B\+PF object pinned to the specified {\itshape pathname}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+A\+T\+T\+A\+CH Description Attach an e\+B\+PF program to a {\itshape target\+\_\+fd} at the specified {\itshape attach\+\_\+type} hook.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape attach\+\_\+type} specifies the e\+B\+PF attachment point to attach the program to, and must be one of {\itshape bpf\+\_\+attach\+\_\+type} (see below).

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape attach\+\_\+bpf\+\_\+fd} must be a valid file descriptor for a loaded e\+B\+PF program of a cgroup, flow dissector, L\+I\+RC, sockmap or sock\+\_\+ops type corresponding to the specified {\itshape attach\+\_\+type}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape target\+\_\+fd} must be a valid file descriptor for a kernel object which depends on the attach type of {\itshape attach\+\_\+bpf\+\_\+fd}\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+D\+E\+V\+I\+CE}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+KB}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+CK}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+A\+D\+DR}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+O\+PT}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+S\+C\+TL}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+O\+PS}} \begin{DoxyVerb}Control Group v2 hierarchy with the eBPF controller
enabled. Requires the kernel to be compiled with
**CONFIG_CGROUP_BPF**.
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+O\+W\+\_\+\+D\+I\+S\+S\+E\+C\+T\+OR}} \begin{DoxyVerb}Network namespace (eg /proc/self/ns/net).
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+L\+I\+R\+C\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E2}} \begin{DoxyVerb}LIRC device path (eg /dev/lircN). Requires the kernel
to be compiled with **CONFIG_BPF_LIRC_MODE2**.
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+K\+\_\+\+S\+KB}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+K\+\_\+\+M\+SG}} \begin{DoxyVerb}eBPF map of socket type (eg **BPF_MAP_TYPE_SOCKHASH**).
\end{DoxyVerb}


Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+D\+E\+T\+A\+CH Description Detach the e\+B\+PF program associated with the {\itshape target\+\_\+fd} at the hook specified by {\itshape attach\+\_\+type}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} program must have been previously attached using {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+A\+T\+T\+A\+CH}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+E\+S\+T\+\_\+\+R\+UN Description Run the e\+B\+PF program associated with the {\itshape prog\+\_\+fd} a {\itshape repeat} number of times against a provided program context {\itshape ctx\+\_\+in} and data {\itshape data\+\_\+in}, and return the modified program context {\itshape ctx\+\_\+out}, {\itshape data\+\_\+out} (for example, packet data), result of the execution {\itshape retval}, and {\itshape duration} of the test run.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} sizes of the buffers provided as input and output parameters {\itshape ctx\+\_\+in}, {\itshape ctx\+\_\+out}, {\itshape data\+\_\+in}, and {\itshape data\+\_\+out} must be provided in the corresponding variables {\itshape ctx\+\_\+size\+\_\+in}, {\itshape ctx\+\_\+size\+\_\+out}, {\itshape data\+\_\+size\+\_\+in}, and/or {\itshape data\+\_\+size\+\_\+out}. If any of these parameters are not provided (ie set to N\+U\+LL), the corresponding size field must be zero.

Some program types have particular requirements\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+K\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+UP}} {\itshape data\+\_\+in} and {\itshape data\+\_\+out} must be N\+U\+LL.

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+R\+A\+W\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+C\+E\+P\+O\+I\+NT}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+R\+A\+W\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+C\+E\+P\+O\+I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+W\+R\+I\+T\+A\+B\+LE}} \begin{DoxyVerb}*ctx_out*, *data_in* and *data_out* must be NULL.
*repeat* must be zero.
\end{DoxyVerb}


Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

{\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+S\+PC}} Either {\itshape data\+\_\+size\+\_\+out} or {\itshape ctx\+\_\+size\+\_\+out} is too small. {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+T\+S\+U\+PP}} This command is not supported by the program type of the program referred to by {\itshape prog\+\_\+fd}.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+ID Description Fetch the next e\+B\+PF program currently loaded into the kernel.

Looks for the e\+B\+PF program with an id greater than {\itshape start\+\_\+id} and updates {\itshape next\+\_\+id} on success. If no other e\+B\+PF programs remain with ids higher than {\itshape start\+\_\+id}, returns -\/1 and sets {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, or when no id remains, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+ID Description Fetch the next e\+B\+PF map currently loaded into the kernel.

Looks for the e\+B\+PF map with an id greater than {\itshape start\+\_\+id} and updates {\itshape next\+\_\+id} on success. If no other e\+B\+PF maps remain with ids higher than {\itshape start\+\_\+id}, returns -\/1 and sets {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, or when no id remains, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+ID Description Open a file descriptor for the e\+B\+PF program corresponding to {\itshape prog\+\_\+id}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+ID Description Open a file descriptor for the e\+B\+PF map corresponding to {\itshape map\+\_\+id}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+I\+N\+F\+O\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+FD Description Obtain information about the e\+B\+PF object corresponding to {\itshape bpf\+\_\+fd}.

Populates up to {\itshape info\+\_\+len} bytes of {\itshape info}, which will be in one of the following formats depending on the e\+B\+PF object type of {\itshape bpf\+\_\+fd}\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structbpf__prog__info}{bpf\+\_\+prog\+\_\+info}}}}
\item {\bfseries{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structbpf__map__info}{bpf\+\_\+map\+\_\+info}}}}
\item {\bfseries{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structbpf__btf__info}{bpf\+\_\+btf\+\_\+info}}}}
\item {\bfseries{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structbpf__link__info}{bpf\+\_\+link\+\_\+info}}}}
\end{DoxyItemize}

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+RY Description Obtain information about e\+B\+PF programs associated with the specified {\itshape attach\+\_\+type} hook.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape target\+\_\+fd} must be a valid file descriptor for a kernel object which depends on the attach type of {\itshape attach\+\_\+bpf\+\_\+fd}\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+D\+E\+V\+I\+CE}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+KB}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+CK}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+A\+D\+DR}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+O\+PT}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+U\+P\+\_\+\+S\+Y\+S\+C\+TL}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+O\+C\+K\+\_\+\+O\+PS}} \begin{DoxyVerb}Control Group v2 hierarchy with the eBPF controller
enabled. Requires the kernel to be compiled with
**CONFIG_CGROUP_BPF**.
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+F\+L\+O\+W\+\_\+\+D\+I\+S\+S\+E\+C\+T\+OR}} \begin{DoxyVerb}Network namespace (eg /proc/self/ns/net).
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+L\+I\+R\+C\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+E2}} \begin{DoxyVerb}LIRC device path (eg /dev/lircN). Requires the kernel
to be compiled with **CONFIG_BPF_LIRC_MODE2**.
\end{DoxyVerb}


{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+RY}} always fetches the number of programs attached and the {\itshape attach\+\_\+flags} which were used to attach those programs. Additionally, if {\itshape prog\+\_\+ids} is nonzero and the number of attached programs is less than {\itshape prog\+\_\+cnt}, populates {\itshape prog\+\_\+ids} with the e\+B\+PF program ids of the programs attached at {\itshape target\+\_\+fd}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} following flags may alter the result\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+R\+Y\+\_\+\+E\+F\+F\+E\+C\+T\+I\+VE}} Only return information regarding programs which are currently effective at the specified {\itshape target\+\_\+fd}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+R\+A\+W\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+C\+E\+P\+O\+I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+O\+P\+EN Description Attach an e\+B\+PF program to a tracepoint {\itshape name} to access kernel internal arguments of the tracepoint in their raw form.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape prog\+\_\+fd} must be a valid file descriptor associated with a loaded e\+B\+PF program of type {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+R\+A\+W\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+C\+E\+P\+O\+I\+NT}}.

No A\+BI guarantees are made about the content of tracepoint arguments exposed to the corresponding e\+B\+PF program.

Applying {\bfseries{close}}\textbackslash{} (2) to the file descriptor returned by {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+R\+A\+W\+\_\+\+T\+R\+A\+C\+E\+P\+O\+I\+N\+T\+\_\+\+O\+P\+EN}} will delete the map (but see N\+O\+T\+ES).

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+B\+T\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD Description Verify and load B\+PF \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} Format (B\+TF) metadata into the kernel, returning a new file descriptor associated with the metadata. B\+TF is described in more detail at \href{https://www.kernel.org/doc/html/latest/bpf/btf.html}{\texttt{ https\+://www.\+kernel.\+org/doc/html/latest/bpf/btf.\+html}}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape btf} parameter must point to valid memory providing {\itshape btf\+\_\+size} bytes of B\+TF binary metadata.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned file descriptor can be passed to other {\bfseries{bpf}}\textbackslash{} () subcommands such as {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD}} or {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE}} to associate the B\+TF with those objects.

Similar to {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD}}, {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+B\+T\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+AD}} has optional parameters to specify a {\itshape btf\+\_\+log\+\_\+buf}, {\itshape btf\+\_\+log\+\_\+size} and {\itshape btf\+\_\+log\+\_\+level} which allow the kernel to return freeform log output regarding the B\+TF verification process.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+B\+T\+F\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+ID Description Open a file descriptor for the B\+PF \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} Format (B\+TF) corresponding to {\itshape btf\+\_\+id}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+T\+A\+S\+K\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+RY Description Obtain information about e\+B\+PF programs associated with the target process identified by {\itshape pid} and {\itshape fd}.

If the {\itshape pid} and {\itshape fd} are associated with a tracepoint, kprobe or uprobe perf event, then the {\itshape prog\+\_\+id} and {\itshape fd\+\_\+type} will be populated with the e\+B\+PF program id and file descriptor type of type {\bfseries{bpf\+\_\+task\+\_\+fd\+\_\+type}}. If associated with a kprobe or uprobe, the {\itshape probe\+\_\+offset} and {\itshape probe\+\_\+addr} will also be populated. Optionally, if {\itshape buf} is provided, then up to {\itshape buf\+\_\+len} bytes of {\itshape buf} will be populated with the name of the tracepoint, kprobe or uprobe.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} resulting {\itshape prog\+\_\+id} may be introspected in deeper detail using {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+ID}} and {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+I\+N\+F\+O\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+FD}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+A\+N\+D\+\_\+\+D\+E\+L\+E\+T\+E\+\_\+\+E\+L\+EM Description Look up an element with the given {\itshape key} in the map referred to by the file descriptor {\itshape fd}, and if found, delete the element.

For {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+UE}} and {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+CK}} map types, the {\itshape flags} argument needs to be set to 0, but for other map types, it may be specified as\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Look up and delete the value of a spin-\/locked map without returning the lock. This must be specified if the elements contain a spinlock.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+UE}} and {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+CK}} map types implement this command as a \char`\"{}pop\char`\"{} operation, deleting the top element rather than one corresponding to {\itshape key}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape key} and {\itshape key\+\_\+len} parameters should be zeroed when issuing this operation for these map types.

This command is only valid for the following map types\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+Q\+U\+E\+UE}}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+CK}}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+H\+A\+SH}}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+C\+P\+U\+\_\+\+H\+A\+SH}}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+L\+R\+U\+\_\+\+H\+A\+SH}}
\item {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+L\+R\+U\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+C\+P\+U\+\_\+\+H\+A\+SH}}
\end{DoxyItemize}

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+F\+R\+E\+E\+ZE Description Freeze the permissions of the specified map.

Write permissions may be frozen by passing zero {\itshape flags}. Upon success, no future syscall invocations may alter the map state of {\itshape map\+\_\+fd}. Write operations from e\+B\+PF programs are still possible for a frozen map.

Not supported for maps of type {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+T\+R\+U\+C\+T\+\_\+\+O\+PS}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+B\+T\+F\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+ID Description Fetch the next B\+PF \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolType-p}{Type}} Format (B\+TF) object currently loaded into the kernel.

Looks for the B\+TF object with an id greater than {\itshape start\+\_\+id} and updates {\itshape next\+\_\+id} on success. If no other B\+TF objects remain with ids higher than {\itshape start\+\_\+id}, returns -\/1 and sets {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, or when no id remains, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH Description Iterate and fetch multiple elements in a map.

Two opaque values are used to manage batch operations, {\itshape in\+\_\+batch} and {\itshape out\+\_\+batch}. Initially, {\itshape in\+\_\+batch} must be set to N\+U\+LL to begin the batched operation. After each subsequent {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH}}, the caller should pass the resultant {\itshape out\+\_\+batch} as the {\itshape in\+\_\+batch} for the next operation to continue iteration from the current point.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape keys} and {\itshape values} are output parameters which must point to memory large enough to hold {\itshape count} items based on the key and value size of the map {\itshape map\+\_\+fd}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape keys} buffer must be of {\itshape key\+\_\+size} $\ast$ {\itshape count}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape values} buffer must be of {\itshape value\+\_\+size} $\ast$ {\itshape count}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape elem\+\_\+flags} argument may be specified as one of the following\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Look up the value of a spin-\/locked map without returning the lock. This must be specified if the elements contain a spinlock.

On success, {\itshape count} elements from the map are copied into the user buffer, with the keys copied into {\itshape keys} and the values copied into the corresponding indices in {\itshape values}.

If an error is returned and {\itshape errno} is not {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, {\itshape count} is set to the number of successfully processed elements.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

May set {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+S\+PC}} to indicate that {\itshape keys} or {\itshape values} is too small to dump an entire bucket during iteration of a hash-\/based map type.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+A\+N\+D\+\_\+\+D\+E\+L\+E\+T\+E\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH Description Iterate and delete all elements in a map.

This operation has the same behavior as {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+L\+O\+O\+K\+U\+P\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH}} with two exceptions\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Every element that is successfully returned is also deleted from the map. This is at least {\itshape count} elements. Note that {\itshape count} is both an input and an output parameter.
\item Upon returning with {\itshape errno} set to {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, up to {\itshape count} elements may be deleted without returning the keys and values of the deleted elements.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+U\+P\+D\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH Description Update multiple elements in a map by {\itshape key}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape keys} and {\itshape values} are input parameters which must point to memory large enough to hold {\itshape count} items based on the key and value size of the map {\itshape map\+\_\+fd}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape keys} buffer must be of {\itshape key\+\_\+size} $\ast$ {\itshape count}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape values} buffer must be of {\itshape value\+\_\+size} $\ast$ {\itshape count}.

Each element specified in {\itshape keys} is sequentially updated to the value in the corresponding index in {\itshape values}. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape in\+\_\+batch} and {\itshape out\+\_\+batch} parameters are ignored and should be zeroed.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape elem\+\_\+flags} argument should be specified as one of the following\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+A\+NY}} Create new elements or update a existing elements. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+N\+O\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} Create new elements only if they do not exist. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} Update existing elements. {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Update spin\+\_\+lock-\/ed map elements. This must be specified if the map value \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} a spinlock.

On success, {\itshape count} elements from the map are updated.

If an error is returned and {\itshape errno} is not {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, {\itshape count} is set to the number of successfully processed elements.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

May set {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+I\+N\+V\+AL}}, {\bfseries{E\+P\+E\+RM}}, {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+M\+EM}}, or {\bfseries{E2\+B\+IG}}. {\bfseries{E2\+B\+IG}} indicates that the number of elements in the map reached the {\itshape max\+\_\+entries} limit specified at map creation time.

May set {\itshape errno} to one of the following error codes under specific circumstances\+:

{\bfseries{E\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} If {\itshape flags} specifies {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+N\+O\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} and the element with {\itshape key} already exists in the map. {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}} If {\itshape flags} specifies {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+E\+X\+I\+ST}} and the element with {\itshape key} does not exist in the map.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+M\+A\+P\+\_\+\+D\+E\+L\+E\+T\+E\+\_\+\+B\+A\+T\+CH Description Delete multiple elements in a map by {\itshape key}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape keys} parameter is an input parameter which must point to memory large enough to hold {\itshape count} items based on the key size of the map {\itshape map\+\_\+fd}, that is, {\itshape key\+\_\+size} $\ast$ {\itshape count}.

Each element specified in {\itshape keys} is sequentially deleted. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape in\+\_\+batch}, {\itshape out\+\_\+batch}, and {\itshape values} parameters are ignored and should be zeroed.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} {\itshape elem\+\_\+flags} argument may be specified as one of the following\+:

{\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+F\+\_\+\+L\+O\+CK}} Look up the value of a spin-\/locked map without returning the lock. This must be specified if the elements contain a spinlock.

On success, {\itshape count} elements from the map are updated.

If an error is returned and {\itshape errno} is not {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, {\itshape count} is set to the number of successfully processed elements. If {\itshape errno} is {\bfseries{E\+F\+A\+U\+LT}}, up to {\itshape count} elements may be been deleted.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE Description Attach an e\+B\+PF program to a {\itshape target\+\_\+fd} at the specified {\itshape attach\+\_\+type} hook and return a file descriptor handle for managing the link.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+U\+P\+D\+A\+TE Description Update the e\+B\+PF program in the specified {\itshape link\+\_\+fd} to {\itshape new\+\_\+prog\+\_\+fd}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+F\+D\+\_\+\+B\+Y\+\_\+\+ID Description Open a file descriptor for the e\+B\+PF Link corresponding to {\itshape link\+\_\+id}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+G\+E\+T\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+ID Description Fetch the next e\+B\+PF link currently loaded into the kernel.

Looks for the e\+B\+PF link with an id greater than {\itshape start\+\_\+id} and updates {\itshape next\+\_\+id} on success. If no other e\+B\+PF links remain with ids higher than {\itshape start\+\_\+id}, returns -\/1 and sets {\itshape errno} to {\bfseries{E\+N\+O\+E\+NT}}.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, or when no id remains, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+E\+N\+A\+B\+L\+E\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+TS Description Enable e\+B\+PF runtime statistics gathering.

Runtime statistics gathering for the e\+B\+PF runtime is disabled by default to minimize the corresponding performance overhead. This command enables statistics globally.

Multiple programs may independently enable statistics. After gathering the desired statistics, e\+B\+PF runtime statistics may be disabled again by calling {\bfseries{close}}\textbackslash{} (2) for the file descriptor returned by this function. Statistics will only be disabled system-\/wide when all outstanding file descriptors returned by prior calls for this subcommand are closed.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+I\+T\+E\+R\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE Description Create an iterator on top of the specified {\itshape link\+\_\+fd} (as previously created using {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+C\+R\+E\+A\+TE}}) and return a file descriptor that can be used to trigger the iteration.

If the resulting file descriptor is pinned to the filesystem using {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+P\+IN}}, then subsequent {\bfseries{read}}\textbackslash{} (2) syscalls for that path will trigger the iterator to read kernel state using the e\+B\+PF program attached to {\itshape link\+\_\+fd}.

Return A new file descriptor (a nonnegative integer), or -\/1 if an error occurred (in which case, {\itshape errno} is set appropriately).

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+D\+E\+T\+A\+CH Description Forcefully detach the specified {\itshape link\+\_\+fd} from its corresponding attachment point.

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+P\+R\+O\+G\+\_\+\+B\+I\+N\+D\+\_\+\+M\+AP Description Bind a map to the lifetime of an e\+B\+PF program.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} map identified by {\itshape map\+\_\+fd} is bound to the program identified by {\itshape prog\+\_\+fd} and only released when {\itshape prog\+\_\+fd} is released. This may be used in cases where metadata should be associated with a program which otherwise does not contain any references to the map (for example, embedded in the e\+B\+PF program instructions).

Return Returns zero on success. On error, -\/1 is returned and {\itshape errno} is set appropriately.

N\+O\+T\+ES e\+B\+PF objects (maps and programs) can be shared between processes.


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item After {\bfseries{fork}}\textbackslash{} (2), the child inherits file descriptors referring to the same e\+B\+PF objects.
\item File descriptors referring to e\+B\+PF objects can be transferred over {\bfseries{unix}}\textbackslash{} (7) domain sockets.
\item File descriptors referring to e\+B\+PF objects can be duplicated in the usual way, using {\bfseries{dup}}\textbackslash{} (2) and similar calls.
\item File descriptors referring to e\+B\+PF objects can be pinned to the filesystem using the {\bfseries{B\+P\+F\+\_\+\+O\+B\+J\+\_\+\+P\+IN}} command of {\bfseries{bpf}}\textbackslash{} (2).
\end{DoxyItemize}

An e\+B\+PF object is deallocated only after all file descriptors referring to the object have been closed and no references remain pinned to the filesystem or attached (for example, bound to a program or device). \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
